Dear Sir/Madam,
--- seg-09e0695c57c52b711b8c0f8f [needs-review] ---
In the wake of the new decade's beginning, in the year that brings all sorts of happiness, I am sending this letter with the hope of illuminating a matter that has long been a subject of concern, apart from the horrors of war.

I wish to propose a course of action regarding the recent observations of unidentified flying objects (UFOs), which have been reported with increasing frequency. These phenomena, while intriguing, have raised numerous questions and concerns.

I have been involved in the study of these objects for several years, and I believe it is time to take a more systematic approach to understanding them. I propose the following:

1. **Documentation and Collection of Data:** I encourage everyone, especially those with access to advanced technology, to document and record their observations. This includes detailed descriptions, photographs, and videos of the UFOs.

2. **Centralized Reporting:** Establish a centralized system for reporting UFO sightings. This will help in organizing and analyzing the data more effectively.

3. **Scientific Investigation:** I suggest forming a committee of scientists, engineers, and astronomers to study the collected data. Their expertise can help interpret the observations and determine their origin.

4. **International Collaboration:** Given the global nature of these phenomena, international collaboration is essential. Sharing information and resources will enhance the overall research effort.

By taking these steps, we can move from mere speculation to a more informed understanding of these mysterious visitors from unknown realms. I am confident that with dedication and scientific rigor, we can unravel the secrets surrounding UFOs.

I look forward to your responses and any additional insights you may have.

Yours sincerely,

[Sender]

The UFO that passed over the Papuda Prison was detected by the radars of the Integrated Air Defense and Air Traffic Control Center (Cindacla), an organ of the Ministry of Aeronautics (located in Lago Sul). Upon encountering the object, Lieutenant Damasceno of the Military Police contacted Sergeant Petrônio, the flight controller at Cindacta, who reported that he was tracking the UFO's movements through the radar.

ii
Thousands of occurrences have been documented:
the passage of Unidentified Flying Objects (UFO) in various places around the world. Not one has been as thoroughly documented as the pursuit of a dozen UFOs by six supersonic F-5 Mirage fighter jets over the cities of São Paulo, São José dos Campos, and Rio de Janeiro on a night in May of 1986. Brazilian pilots attempted to intercept the strange lights moving at extremely high speed, but their attempts were unsuccessful and they became the pursued rather than the pursuers.

More than a mere collective hallucination, the episode of that night became...
--- seg-a65a87050c30ac7c20cc2b27 [machine-unreviewed] ---
; li
--- seg-02d0e6bddfefaa21092a14fe [needs-review] ---
Lima, during a press conference three days
after, spoke about the presence of a wave of
flying objects, stating that "the government
has nothing to hide."

The fighter jet pilots (from Anápolis / Goiás and Santa Cruz / Rio de Janeiro) said in their statements that "a multi-colored light" surrounded an F-1, and Brigadier Moreira stated that for several minutes, the objects pursued the planes and then suddenly disappeared. Prior to the pilots, the then-president of the Institute, Oirces Silva, reportedly pursued the 'discs' for approximately 30 minutes. When he landed, a São José dos Campos resident, Oirces, informed the authorities.

5 Aerial.
Brasília — The first UFO seen
in Brasília dates back to 1959, when
father Raimundo Nascimento Teixeira
gathered, in Núcleo Bandeirante, a
crowd to observe a "disc-shaped object
that moved at great speed," according to
some mystics, Brasília receives periodic
visits from aliens. Here, until recently,
under the coordination of General Alfredo
Mõaeyr de Mendonça Uchoa — currently
ill — operated the National Center for
Ufoological Studies.
On the night of May 18, 1986, composer
José Dantas saw in Sector P South
(Toguatinga) a very bright, mushroom-
shaped light in the distance. Dantas cal-
culated that the light lasted for about
three minutes and thought it was "a spa-
ce ship from another planet."

The trekmanians are spread all over
the world and claim that NASA collected
(and stored, by freezing) the bodies of
extraterrestrials victims of a UFO acci-
dent in 1947, near a U.S. air base in New
Mexico.

--- seg-5063cde5da8eb48ec2d01655 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Unidentified flying object (UFO) sightings have been reported throughout history, with various descriptions and alleged encounters. These reports often spark curiosity, speculation, and debates about their origins. While some believe they could be evidence of extraterrestrial life, others attribute them to natural phenomena, human technology, or psychological explanations.

The term "UFO" refers to any aerial phenomenon that cannot be immediately identified or explained. It does not inherently imply extraterrestrial origin. Reports of UFOs range from bright lights in the sky to strange objects or shapes that seem to defy conventional flight dynamics.

Many UFO sightings remain unexplained, leading to ongoing investigations and discussions within scientific and paranormal communities. Some cases have gained significant attention from the media and the public, often fueling conspiracy theories and speculation about hidden government knowledge.

0 Group of UFO Studies - GEU, from the University of Brasília, is investigating data related to the incident that occurred in the proximity of the International and Reintegration Center - CIR, known as Papuda, on the last day 04.09, as reported by the press.

In order to complement these data, we request this Center to provide information on possible meteorological balloon emissions during the period from 03.04 to 20.04.09.

If possible, please provide us with emission data and times, recovery data and locations of the equipment, as well as the type utilized. We would also like to know if these balloons are being filled with helium or hydrogen; if they carry any type of lamp and, finally, according to the usual or anomalous conditions, whether they are subject to emission of some type of luminescence through gas ionization or electrostatic charging of the particles in the statistical cloud.

In Brazil
This publication you are reading is
the only Brazilian specialized magazine
in the scientific research of Unidentified Flying Objects (UFOs or UFOs), also known as "flying saucers". UFO is maintained by the largest national and international research center, the Brazilian Center for UFO Research (JCBPDV). With its headquarters in Campo Grande (MS) and over 5,000 members spread throughout Brazil and 32 other countries. Until this year, the UFO magazine was edited by the Paracientific Editorial Group and only counted on the technical responsibility of the Research Center, known as CPOV.

UFO is an objective dissemination magazine, absolutely dedicated to a scientifically-based approach to the UFO phenomenon. All those interested in participating in its development can contribute with texts, theses, articles, etc. Those who wish to get involved in the study of UFOs can still associate with the JCBPDV, receiving the magazine every month, along with a variety of regular research updates. To do so, simply fill out the coupon on the central pages. Similarly, the JCBPDV and the UFO magazine offer a wide range of urological research materials from various sources, always announced in the publication.

To obtain them, "just fill out and send the respective coupons."

After year upon year, observations of disc-shaped objects in Brazil • just as in the rest of the world • have been steadily increasing. Not traditionally 'preferred' areas by extraterrestrials, rural regions have been constant targets of constant man-made efforts, purposes, overviews, and contacts with these beings, but also large cities have been consistently "visited" - something that was well rare some time ago. This clearly indicates that our visitors have taken off their masks once and for all and are giving out samples of their inexhaustible interest in human actions and affairs. However, humanity, alas, seems to make little effort to accompany and engage more intimately in this issue, as would be expected. And worse yet, the world authorities' handling of this phenomenon also leaves much to be desired: for a phenomenon of such magnitude, like the UFO phenomenon, one would expect the world powers, both civil and military, to have the responsibility not only to keep a complete track of these visitors' actions on Earth, but also - and much more importantly - to keep large programs of disclosure of the facts to all sectors of the planetary society, so that its members are not "taken by surprise" in case 'something' happens...

Particularly, Brazil's actions in this regard seem to be more timid. Our authorities have excelled first in research and tracking of these beings' incursions into national territory, but it seems they have forgotten their second task: to disclose the facts to the population...

So far, we - the people - have not had the pleasure of seeing even a single Brazilian authority give explanations for so many occurrences. Nor does this same people have anything to do with this same authority, here represented by the only publication on the subject (which, in turn, is the voice of a whole community of ufologists and researchers of the phenomenon of the disc-shaped objects in the whole country). To give a 'modest' contribution to the work of our authorities - as we have always done - in divulging their research on the subject! With this, their mission is completed, and so is ours.

A. J. Gevaerd, editor.

--- seg-4611cd8bab75e676f5681194 [needs-review] ---
In 1988, after several series of UFO publications that we launched, when we began the edition of the results of these experiences and editorial work, translated in the UFO magazine, it became clear that the subject of these discussions required a complete and absolutely efficient coverage. It also became clear that a single publication could not be so comprehensive: it was necessary to create parallel UFO series to support it and to better capture a comprehensive movement of self-promotion. Thus, two new series were conceived: UFO Special, to deal with specific aspects, and more in-depth.
--- seg-5d4a34b449eb654d067a05f8 [needs-review] ---
The UFOlogy documents, generally focus
on complex themes that cannot be addressed
in a regular issue, and UFO Document, dedicated
to publishing official or historical themes
of UFOlogy. In order to maintain the 'memory'
of everything that occurs in the field. The special series
was edited up to issue 3, covering topics
such as the typology of alien humanoids,
the complete report of the Vasp 169 case,
and analyses of UFO photos. The document
series had only its first issue published,
justly about the International UFOlogy
Congress in Rio de Janeiro (1988). Both
series were discontinued in 1989 due to
the massive Brazilian inflation that made
them unviable, ceasing to circulate. Now,
apparently surpassing this phase, UFO Special
is returning to newsstands next week,
with an unprecedented issue on UFO photos,
and UFO Document is now returning with
this second edition, exclusively about
official research of the FAB. Both series
will now circulate quarterly.

The text that begins below is being printed, in this edition, without any improvements, edits, corrections, or adaptations. It is the integral reproduction of the mission actions report of the so-called "Operation Prato", developed by teams from the 1st Regional Air Command (COMAR), of the Brazilian Air Force, headquartered in Belém, Pará. The only alteration made to the document, presented here in the form of a facsimile, was the enlargement of its header and its printing in different typefaces than those used in the original. Similarly, some illegible lines in the original document were redacted and replaced, without any alteration to the content (Editor).

DESCRIPTION OF THE OBJECT
M
--- seg-dbad2ae1d13dbf6eb32d0440 [needs-review] ---
A distance, it resembled a streak of yellow-red light; then it turned a clear yellow color, and as it neared, it was observed to have a glowing, blue-colored front (45°) that emitted a blue-glowing light. Its apparent size is 1,20/1,40m, allowing for transmission (blue-glowing part) with a division between its passengers.

Movement; Retreating with a undulating motion (leaf in the wind), it then adjusts a certain height, at a rapid velocity (impulses) with abrupt changes. Upon reaching the desired height, it has the larger diameter tube as its rear, emitting a glowing light. The same occurs during its descent. This produces a strong sound, sending a visual impression (like a bicycle wheel).

Eyes: Projecting, tubular shape.

unpublished in Brazil, of debates, and of official discussions about UFO sightings and their consequences for the world, with emphasis on the United States. By the author and researcher (name redacted).

The text presents a collection of UFO-related documents, including detailed accounts of three verified incidents, as well as official discussions and evidence from around the world. It offers an unprecedented look at the subject, previously unpublished, from the perspective of a researcher.

--- seg-385de5327cc5579e3fd04579 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Every day, the Pantanal is suffering from increasingly deep and constant attacks that could lead to its destruction and the loss of its riches, fauna, and flora. Predatory fishing, criminal and impune hunting, the killing of jacarés for the illegal extraction of their skin, the indiscriminate use of insecticides and other agrochemicals, uncontrolled tourism, and the negligence of state and federal authorities all condemn this sanctuary to death. Something must be done before it's too late. Know it and fight for its preservation! It's still too soon...
